ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\n    20→\n    21→import numpy as np\n    22→import pandas as pd\n    23→import h5py\n    24→\n    25→warnings.filterwarnings('ignore', category=FutureWarning)\n    26→\n    27→# ============================================================================\n    28→# Constants\n    29→# ============================================================================\n    30→TARGET_RATE_HZ = 30.0  # Paper: \"linearly interpolating onto a consistent set of 30hz timestamps\"\n    31→TIME_BIN_MS = 1000.0 / TARGET_RATE_HZ  # ~33.33 ms\n    32→\n    33→DATA_DIR = Path('data/visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0')\n    34→NWB_DIR = DATA_DIR / 'behavior_ophys_experiments'\n    35→META_DIR = DATA_DIR / 'project_metadata'\n    36→\n    37→# Session types to include (active behavior only, exclude passive)\n    38→ACTIVE_SESSION_TYPES = [\n    39→    'OPHYS_1_images_A', 'OPHYS_3_images_A',\n    40→    'OPHYS_4_images_B', 'OPHYS_6_images_B',\n    41→]\n    42→\n    43→# ============================================================================\n    44→# Helper Functions\n    45→# ============================================================================\n    46→\n    47→def interpolate_to_regular_grid(timestamps, data, target_timestamps):\n    48→    \"\"\"Linearly interpolate data from irregular timestamps to regular grid.\n    49→\n    50→    Args:\n    51→        timestamps: (n_orig,) original timestamps\n    52→        data: (n_orig,) or (n_orig, n_features) data array\n    53→        target_timestamps: (n_target,) target timestamps\n    54→\n    55→    Returns:\n    56→        interpolated data of shape (n_target,) or (n_target, n_features)\n    57→    \"\"\"\n    58→    if data.ndim == 1:\n    59→        return np.interp(target_timestamps, timestamps, data)\n    60→    else:\n    61→        result = np.zeros((len(target_timestamps), data.shape[1]), dtype=np.float32)\n    62→        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n    63→            result[:, i] = np.interp(target_timestamps, timestamps, data[:, i])\n    64→        return result\n    65→\n    66→\n    67→def compute_percentile_bins(values, n_bins=5):\n    68→    \"\"\"Compute bin edges from equal percentiles, ignoring NaN.\n    69→\n    70→    Returns:\n    71→        bin_edges: array of n_bins+1 edges\n    72→    \"\"\"\n    73→    valid = values[~np.isnan(values)]\n    74→    if len(valid) == 0:\n    75→        return np.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\n    76→    percentiles = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)\n    77→    edges = np.percentile(valid, percentiles)\n    78→    # Ensure unique edges (handle constant values)\n    79→    # Add small epsilon to make edges strictly increasing\n    80→    for i in range(1, len(edges)):\n    81→        if edges[i] <= edges[i-1]:\n    82→            edges[i] = edges[i-1] + 1e-10\n    83→    return edges\n    84→\n    85→\n    86→def digitize_to_bins(values, bin_edges):\n    87→    \"\"\"Digitize values into bins defined by bin_edges.\n    88→\n    89→    Returns integer bin indices from 0 to n_bins-1.\n    90→    NaN values get bin 0.\n    91→    \"\"\"\n    92→    n_bins = len(bin_edges) - 1\n    93→    binned = np.digitize(values, bin_edges[1:-1])  # 0 to n_bins-1\n    94→    binned = np.clip(binned, 0, n_bins - 1)\n    95→    # NaN handling\n    96→    binned[np.isnan(values)] = 0\n    97→    return binned.astype(np.int64)\n    98→\n    99→\n   100→def interpolate_nans(arr):\n   101→    \"\"\"Linearly interpolate NaN values in a 1D array.\"\"\"\n   102→    nans = np.isnan(arr)\n   103→    if not nans.any():\n   104→        return arr.copy()\n   105→    if nans.all():\n   106→        return np.zeros_like(arr)\n   107→    result = arr.copy()\n   108→    x = np.arange(len(arr))\n   109→    result[nans] = np.interp(x[nans], x[~nans], arr[~nans])\n   110→    return result\n   111→\n   112→\n   113→# ============================================================================\n   114→# NWB Loading Functions\n   115→# ============================================================================\n   116→\n   117→def load_experiment_data(nwb_path, experiment_id):\n   118→    \"\"\"Load all needed data from a single NWB file.\n   119→\n   120→    Returns a dict with all extracted data, or None if loading fails.\n   121→    \"\"\"\n   122→    try:\n   123→        with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n   124→            # --- Cell specimen table ---\n   125→            cell_table = f['processing']['ophys']['image_segmentation']['cell_specimen_table']\n   126→            valid_roi = cell_table['valid_roi'][()].astype(bool)\n   127→            cell_specimen_ids = cell_table['cell_specimen_id'][()]\n   128→\n   129→            n_valid = valid_roi.sum()\n   130→            if n_valid == 0:\n   131→                print(f\"  WARNING: No valid ROIs in experiment {experiment_id}, skipping\")\n   132→                return None\n   133→\n   134→            # --- Ophys timestamps ---\n   135→            ophys_ts = f['processing']['ophys']['dff']['traces']['timestamps'][()]\n   136→\n   137→            # --- Neural events (filtered by valid_roi) ---\n   138→            events_data = f['processing']['ophys']['event_detection']['data'][()]\n   139→            # events_data shape: (n_timepoints, n_all_cells)\n   140→            events_valid = events_data[:, valid_roi]  # (n_timepoints, n_valid_cells)\n   141→\n   142→            # --- Trials ---\n   143→            trials_grp = f['intervals']['trials']\n   144→            n_trials = len(trials_grp['start_time'][()])\n   145→            trials = {\n   146→                'start_time': trials_grp['start_time'][()],\n   147→                'stop_time': trials_grp['stop_time'][()],\n   148→                'change_time': trials_grp['change_time'][()],\n   149→                'go': trials_grp['go'][()].astype(bool),\n   150→                'catch': trials_grp['catch'][()].astype(bool),\n   151→                'aborted': trials_grp['aborted'][()].astype(bool),\n   152→                'auto_rewarded': trials_grp['auto_rewarded'][()].astype(bool),\n   153→                'hit': trials_grp['hit'][()].astype(bool),\n   154→                'miss': trials_grp['miss'][()].astype(bool),\n   155→                'false_alarm': trials_grp['false_alarm'][()].astype(bool),\n   156→                'correct_reject': trials_grp['correct_reject'][()].astype(bool),\n   157→                'is_change': trials_grp['is_change'][()].astype(bool),\n   158→                'initial_image_name': trials_grp['initial_image_name'][()],\n   159→                'change_image_name': trials_grp['change_image_name'][()],\n   160→            }\n   161→            # Decode bytes\n   162→            if isinstance(trials['initial_image_name'][0], bytes):\n   163→                trials['initial_image_name'] = np.array([x.decode() for x in trials['initial_image_name']])\n   164→                trials['change_image_name'] = np.array([x.decode() for x in trials['change_image_name']])\n   165→\n   166→            # --- Stimulus presentations ---\n   167→            # Find the natural images presentation table\n   168→            stim_key = None\n   169→            for k in f['intervals'].keys():\n   170→                if k != 'trials' and 'spontaneous' not in k.lower() and 'movie' not in k.lower():\n   171→                    stim_key = k\n   172→                    break\n   173→\n   174→            if stim_key is None:\n   175→                print(f\"  WARNING: No stimulus presentations found in {experiment_id}, skipping\")\n   176→                return None\n   177→\n   178→            stim = f['intervals'][stim_key]\n   179→            stim_data = {\n   180→                'start_time': stim['start_time'][()],\n   181→                'stop_time': stim['stop_time'][()],\n   182→                'image_name': stim['image_name'][()],\n   183→                'is_change': stim['is_change'][()].astype(bool),\n   184→                'omitted': stim['omitted'][()],\n   185→            }\n   186→            if isinstance(stim_data['image_name'][0], bytes):\n   187→                stim_data['image_name'] = np.array([x.decode() for x in stim_data['image_name']])\n   188→\n   189→            # Handle omitted field (might be float)\n   190→            if stim_data['omitted'].dtype == float:\n   191→                stim_data['omitted'] = stim_data['omitted'].astype(bool)\n   192→\n   193→            # --- Running speed ---\n   194→            running_speed = f['processing']['running']['speed']['data'][()]\n   195→            running_ts = f['processing']['running']['speed']['timestamps'][()]\n   196→\n   197→            # --- Pupil tracking ---\n   198→            pupil_area = None\n   199→            pupil_ts = None\n   200→            likely_blink = None\n   201→            try:\n   202→                pupil_tracking = f['acquisition']['EyeTracking']['pupil_tracking']\n   203→                pupil_area = pupil_tracking['area'][()]\n   204→                pupil_ts = pupil_tracking['timestamps'][()]\n   205→                likely_blink = f['acquisition']['EyeTracking']['likely_blink']['data'][()].astype(bool)\n   206→            except (KeyError, Exception):\n   207→                print(f\"  WARNING: No pupil tracking data in {experiment_id}\")\n   208→\n   209→            return {\n   210→                'experiment_id': experiment_id,\n   211→                'valid_roi': valid_roi,\n   212→                'cell_specimen_ids': cell_specimen_ids[valid_roi],\n   213→                'n_valid_cells': n_valid,\n   214→                'ophys_ts': ophys_ts,\n   215→                'events': events_valid,\n   216→                'trials': trials,\n   217→                'stim': stim_data,\n   218→                'running_speed': running_speed,\n   219→                'running_ts': running_ts,\n   220→                'pupil_area': pupil_area,\n   221→                'pupil_ts': pupil_ts,\n   222→                'likely_blink': likely_blink,\n   223→            }\n   224→    except Exception as e:\n   225→        print(f\"  ERROR loading {experiment_id}: {e}\")\n   226→        return None\n   227→\n   228→\n   229→# ============================================================================\n   230→# Trial Processing\n   231→# ============================================================================\n   232→\n   233→def get_image_at_timepoints(timepoints, stim_data, all_image_names):\n   234→    \"\"\"For each timepoint, determine which image is currently being shown.\n   235→\n   236→    Returns array of image indices (into all_image_names) for each timepoint.\n   237→    During gray screen periods, returns the last shown image.\n   238→    For omitted flashes, continues with the previous image.\n   239→    \"\"\"\n   240→    n_tp = len(timepoints)\n   241→    image_idx = np.zeros(n_tp, dtype=np.int64)\n   242→\n   243→    # Build a sorted list of (start_time, image_name) for non-omitted stimuli\n   244→    non_omitted = ~stim_data['omitted']\n   245→    stim_starts = stim_data['start_time'][non_omitted]\n   246→    stim_names = stim_data['image_name'][non_omitted]\n   247→\n   248→    # Map image names to indices\n   249→    name_to_idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(all_image_names)}\n   250→\n   251→    # For each timepoint, find the most recent stimulus onset\n   252→    # Use searchsorted for efficiency\n   253→    insert_idx = np.searchsorted(stim_starts, timepoints, side='right') - 1\n   254→\n   255→    for i in range(n_tp):\n   256→        if insert_idx[i] >= 0:\n   257→            img_name = stim_names[insert_idx[i]]\n   258→            image_idx[i] = name_to_idx.get(img_name, 0)\n   259→        else:\n   260→            image_idx[i] = 0  # Before first stimulus\n   261→\n   262→    return image_idx\n   263→\n   264→\n   265→def get_image_change_at_timepoints(timepoints, stim_data):\n   266→    \"\"\"For each timepoint, determine if an image change is occurring.\n   267→\n   268→    Returns binary array: 1 during the first flash after a change, 0 otherwise.\n   269→    \"\"\"\n   270→    n_tp = len(timepoints)\n   271→    change_signal = np.zeros(n_tp, dtype=np.int64)\n   272→\n   273→    # Get change stimulus presentations (non-omitted, is_change=True)\n   274→    change_mask = stim_data['is_change'] & ~stim_data['omitted']\n   275→    change_starts = stim_data['start_time'][change_mask]\n   276→    change_stops = stim_data['stop_time'][change_mask]\n   277→\n   278→    # For each change, mark timepoints within the 750ms image presentation interval\n   279→    for cs, ce in zip(change_starts, change_stops):\n   280→        # Mark the full image interval (stimulus + gray) as change\n   281→        # Use 750ms window from change start\n   282→        mask = (timepoints >= cs) & (timepoints < cs + 0.75)\n   283→        change_signal[mask] = 1\n   284→\n   285→    return change_signal\n   286→\n   287→\n   288→def process_single_experiment(raw_data, exp_meta, target_rate=TARGET_RATE_HZ):\n   289→    \"\"\"Process a single experiment into trial-segmented format.\n   290→\n   291→    Args:\n   292→        raw_data: dict from load_experiment_data\n   293→        exp_meta: dict with 'targeted_structure', 'mouse_id' etc.\n   294→        target_rate: target sampling rate in Hz\n   295→\n   296→    Returns:\n   297→        dict with processed trial data, or None if processing fails.\n   298→    \"\"\"\n   299→    trials = raw_data['trials']\n   300→\n   301→    # Filter trials: keep Go and Catch, exclude Aborted and Auto-rewarded\n   302→    valid_mask = (trials['go'] | trials['catch']) & ~trials['aborted'] & ~trials['auto_rewarded']\n   303→    valid_indices = np.where(valid_mask)[0]\n   304→\n   305→    if len(valid_indices) < 2:\n   306→        print(f\"  WARNING: Only {len(valid_indices)} valid trials in experiment {raw_data['experiment_id']}, skipping\")\n   307→        return None\n   308→\n   309→    # --- Resample neural events to target rate ---\n   310→    ophys_ts = raw_data['ophys_ts']\n   311→    events = raw_data['events']  # (n_timepoints, n_cells)\n   312→\n   313→    # Create regular timestamp grid at target rate\n   314→    t_start = ophys_ts[0]\n   315→    t_end = ophys_ts[-1]\n   316→    dt = 1.0 / target_rate\n   317→    regular_ts = np.arange(t_start, t_end, dt)\n   318→\n   319→    # Interpolate events to regular grid\n   320→    events_resampled = interpolate_to_regular_grid(ophys_ts, events, regular_ts)\n   321→    # Ensure non-negative (events should be >= 0)\n   322→    events_resampled = np.maximum(events_resampled, 0)\n   323→\n   324→    # --- Resample running speed ---\n   325→    running_resampled = np.interp(regular_ts, raw_data['running_ts'], raw_data['running_speed'])\n   326→\n   327→    # --- Resample pupil area ---\n   328→    pupil_resampled = None\n   329→    if raw_data['pupil_area'] is not None:\n   330→        pupil_area = raw_data['pupil_area'].copy().astype(float)\n   331→        pupil_ts = raw_data['pupil_ts']\n   332→        likely_blink = raw_data['likely_blink']\n   333→\n   334→        # Set blink frames to NaN\n   335→        if likely_blink is not None:\n   336→            pupil_area[likely_blink] = np.nan\n   337→\n   338→        # Interpolate NaNs in pupil area before resampling\n   339→        pupil_area = interpolate_nans(pupil_area)\n   340→\n   341→        # Resample to regular grid\n   342→        pupil_resampled = np.interp(regular_ts, pupil_ts, pupil_area)\n   343→\n   344→    # --- Get all unique image names (excluding 'omitted') ---\n   345→    all_stim_images = sorted(set(\n   346→        raw_data['stim']['image_name'][raw_data['stim']['image_name'] != 'omitted']\n   347→    ))\n   348→\n   349→    # --- Process each valid trial ---\n   350→    neural_trials = []\n   351→    output_trials = []\n   352→    trial_outcomes = []\n   353→\n   354→    for trial_idx in valid_indices:\n   355→        t_trial_start = trials['start_time'][trial_idx]\n   356→        t_trial_stop = trials['stop_time'][trial_idx]\n   357→\n   358→        # Find regular grid indices within this trial\n   359→        trial_mask = (regular_ts >= t_trial_start) & (regular_ts < t_trial_stop)\n   360→        trial_time_indices = np.where(trial_mask)[0]\n   361→\n   362→        if len(trial_time_indices) < 3:\n   363→            continue\n   364→\n   365→        trial_ts = regular_ts[trial_time_indices]\n   366→        n_tp = len(trial_ts)\n   367→\n   368→        # Neural data: (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n   369→        neural_trial = events_resampled[trial_time_indices, :].T.astype(np.float32)\n   370→\n   371→        # Image identity at each timepoint\n   372→        image_idx = get_image_at_timepoints(trial_ts, raw_data['stim'], all_stim_images)\n   373→\n   374→        # Image change signal\n   375→        change_signal = get_image_change_at_timepoints(trial_ts, raw_data['stim'])\n   376→\n   377→        # Running speed (already resampled)\n   378→        running_trial = running_resampled[trial_time_indices]\n   379→\n   380→        # Pupil area (already resampled)\n   381→        if pupil_resampled is not None:\n   382→            pupil_trial = pupil_resampled[trial_time_indices]\n   383→        else:\n   384→            pupil_trial = np.full(n_tp, np.nan)\n   385→\n   386→        # Trial outcome (static)\n   387→        if trials['hit'][trial_idx]:\n   388→            outcome = 0  # hit\n   389→        elif trials['miss'][trial_idx]:\n   390→            outcome = 1  # miss\n   391→        elif trials['false_alarm'][trial_idx]:\n   392→            outcome = 2  # false_alarm\n   393→        elif trials['correct_reject'][trial_idx]:\n   394→            outcome = 3  # correct_reject\n   395→        else:\n   396→            continue  # Unknown outcome, skip\n   397→\n   398→        neural_trials.append(neural_trial)\n   399→        # Store raw values for now; discretization happens after collecting all data\n   400→        output_trials.append({\n   401→            'image_identity': image_idx,\n   402→            'image_change': change_signal,\n   403→            'running_speed': running_trial.astype(np.float32),\n   404→            'pupil_area': pupil_trial.astype(np.float32),\n   405→            'trial_outcome': outcome,\n   406→        })\n   407→\n   408→    if len(neural_trials) < 2:\n   409→        print(f\"  WARNING: Only {len(neural_trials)} processed trials in experiment {raw_data['experiment_id']}, skipping\")\n   410→        return None\n   411→\n   412→    return {\n   413→        'experiment_id': raw_data['experiment_id'],\n   414→        'neural_trials': neural_trials,\n   415→        'output_trials': output_trials,\n   416→        'n_cells': raw_data['n_valid_cells'],\n   417→        'all_image_names': all_stim_images,\n   418→        'targeted_structure': exp_meta['targeted_structure'],\n   419→        'mouse_id': str(exp_meta['mouse_id']),\n   420→    }\n   421→\n   422→\n   423→# ============================================================================\n   424→# Main Conversion\n   425→# ============================================================================\n   426→\n   427→def get_experiment_list(sample=False):\n   428→    \"\"\"Get list of active experiment IDs with metadata.\"\"\"\n   429→    exp_table = pd.read_csv(META_DIR / 'ophys_experiment_table.csv')\n   430→\n   431→    # Get NWB files on disk\n   432→    nwb_files = list(NWB_DIR.glob('*.nwb'))\n   433→    nwb_ids = set()\n   434→    for f in nwb_files:\n   435→        try:\n   436→            eid = int(f.stem.split('_')[-1])\n   437→            nwb_ids.add(eid)\n   438→        except ValueError:\n   439→            pass\n   440→\n   441→    # Filter to on-disk, active sessions\n   442→    mask = (\n   443→        exp_table['ophys_experiment_id'].isin(nwb_ids) &\n   444→        exp_table['session_type'].isin(ACTIVE_SESSION_TYPES)\n   445→    )\n   446→    active_exps = exp_table[mask].copy()\n   447→\n   448→    if sample:\n   449→        # Pick 2 sessions (preferably from different mice, different equipment)\n   450→        # Pick one single-plane and one multi-plane if possible\n   451→        single = active_exps[active_exps['equipment_name'].str.startswith('CAM')]\n   452→        if len(single) > 0:\n   453→            first = single.iloc[0]\n   454→        else:\n   455→            first = active_exps.iloc[0]\n   456→\n   457→        remaining = active_exps[active_exps['mouse_id'] != first['mouse_id']]\n   458→        if len(remaining) > 0:\n   459→            second = remaining.iloc[0]\n   460→        else:\n   461→            second = active_exps.iloc[1] if len(active_exps) > 1 else first\n   462→\n   463→        active_exps = active_exps[\n   464→            active_exps['ophys_experiment_id'].isin([\n   465→                first['ophys_experiment_id'],\n   466→                second['ophys_experiment_id']\n   467→            ])\n   468→        ]\n   469→\n   470→    print(f\"Selected {len(active_exps)} experiments from {active_exps['mouse_id'].nunique()} mice\")\n   471→    return active_exps\n   472→\n   473→\n   474→def convert_all(exp_table, show_processing=False):\n   475→    \"\"\"Convert all experiments to the target format.\"\"\"\n   476→\n   477→    all_neural = []\n   478→    all_input = []\n   479→    all_output = []\n   480→    all_subject_idx = []\n   481→    all_brain_region_idx = []\n   482→\n   483→    subjects = sorted(exp_table['mouse_id'].unique().astype(str))\n   484→    subject_to_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   485→\n   486→    brain_regions = sorted(exp_table['targeted_structure'].unique())\n   487→    region_to_idx = {r: i for i, r in enumerate(brain_regions)}\n   488→\n   489→    # Collect all image names across experiments to build a global mapping\n   490→    all_image_names_set = set()\n   491→\n   492→    # First pass: determine global image names\n   493→    print(\"\\n--- Pass 1: Collecting global image names ---\")\n   494→    t_start_global = time.time()\n   495→\n   496→    # Quick scan of a few files to get image names\n   497→    for _, row in exp_table.iterrows():\n   498→        eid = row['ophys_experiment_id']\n   499→        nwb_path = NWB_DIR / f'behavior_ophys_experiment_{eid}.nwb'\n   500→        try:\n   501→            with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n   502→                for k in f['intervals'].keys():\n   503→                    if k != 'trials' and 'spontaneous' not in k.lower() and 'movie' not in k.lower():\n   504→                        img_names = f['intervals'][k]['image_name'][()]\n   505→                        if isinstance(img_names[0], bytes):\n   506→                            img_names = [x.decode() for x in img_names]\n   507→                        all_image_names_set.update([n for n in img_names if n != 'omitted'])\n   508→                        break\n   509→        except Exception as e:\n   510→            print(f\"  Error scanning {eid}: {e}\")\n   511→\n   512→    global_image_names = sorted(all_image_names_set)\n   513→    print(f\"  Global image names ({len(global_image_names)}): {global_image_names}\")\n   514→\n   515→    # --- Collect all running speed and pupil values for global percentile computation ---\n   516→    print(\"\\n--- Pass 2: Collecting running speed and pupil data for percentile bins ---\")\n   517→    all_running_values = []\n   518→    all_pupil_values = []\n   519→\n   520→    for idx, (_, row) in enumerate(exp_table.iterrows()):\n   521→        eid = row['ophys_experiment_id']\n   522→        nwb_path = NWB_DIR / f'behavior_ophys_experiment_{eid}.nwb'\n   523→        try:\n   524→            with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n   525→                running = f['processing']['running']['speed']['data'][()]\n   526→                all_running_values.append(running.astype(np.float32))\n   527→\n   528→                try:\n   529→ python3\n\"\"\"\nConvert Allen Brain Observatory Visual Behavior 2P data to decoder-compatible format.\n\nUsage:\n    python -u convert_data.py <outpicklefile> [--full|--sample] [--show-processing]\n\nThe script loads NWB experiment files, extracts neural events, behavioral data,\nand trial structure, then saves in the standardized decoder format.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport pickle\nimport warnings\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport h5py\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore', category=FutureWarning)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Constants\n# ============================================================================\nTARGET_RATE_HZ = 30.0  # Paper: \"linearly interpolating onto a consistent set of 30hz timestamps\"\nTIME_BIN_MS = 1000.0 / TARGET_RATE_HZ  # ~33.33 ms\n\nDATA_DIR = Path('data/visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0')\nNWB_DIR = DATA_DIR / 'behavior_ophys_experiments'\nMETA_DIR = DATA_DIR / 'project_metadata'\n\n# Session types to include (active behavior only, exclude passive)\nACTIVE_SESSION_TYPES = [\n    'OPHYS_1_images_A', 'OPHYS_3_images_A',\n    'OPHYS_4_images_B', 'OPHYS_6_images_B',\n]\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef interpolate_to_regular_grid(timestamps, data, target_timestamps):\n    \"\"\"Linearly interpolate data from irregular timestamps to regular grid.\n\n    Args:\n        timestamps: (n_orig,) original timestamps\n        data: (n_orig,) or (n_orig, n_features) data array\n        target_timestamps: (n_target,) target timestamps\n\n    Returns:\n        interpolated data of shape (n_target,) or (n_target, n_features)\n    \"\"\"\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        return np.interp(target_timestamps, timestamps, data)\n    else:\n        result = np.zeros((len(target_timestamps), data.shape[1]), dtype=np.float32)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            result[:, i] = np.interp(target_timestamps, timestamps, data[:, i])\n        return result\n\n\ndef compute_percentile_bins(values, n_bins=5):\n    \"\"\"Compute bin edges from equal percentiles, ignoring NaN.\n\n    Returns:\n        bin_edges: array of n_bins+1 edges\n    \"\"\"\n    valid = values[~np.isnan(values)]\n    if len(valid) == 0:\n        return np.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\n    percentiles = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)\n    edges = np.percentile(valid, percentiles)\n    # Ensure unique edges (handle constant values)\n    # Add small epsilon to make edges strictly increasing\n    for i in range(1, len(edges)):\n        if edges[i] <= edges[i-1]:\n            edges[i] = edges[i-1] + 1e-10\n    return edges\n\n\ndef digitize_to_bins(values, bin_edges):\n    \"\"\"Digitize values into bins defined by bin_edges.\n\n    Returns integer bin indices from 0 to n_bins-1.\n    NaN values get bin 0.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_bins = len(bin_edges) - 1\n    binned = np.digitize(values, bin_edges[1:-1])  # 0 to n_bins-1\n    binned = np.clip(binned, 0, n_bins - 1)\n    # NaN handling\n    binned[np.isnan(values)] = 0\n    return binned.astype(np.int64)\n\n\ndef interpolate_nans(arr):\n    \"\"\"Linearly interpolate NaN values in a 1D array.\"\"\"\n    nans = np.isnan(arr)\n    if not nans.any():\n        return arr.copy()\n    if nans.all():\n        return np.zeros_like(arr)\n    result = arr.copy()\n    x = np.arange(len(arr))\n    result[nans] = np.interp(x[nans], x[~nans], arr[~nans])\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# NWB Loading Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef load_experiment_data(nwb_path, experiment_id):\n    \"\"\"Load all needed data from a single NWB file.\n\n    Returns a dict with all extracted data, or None if loading fails.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n            # --- Cell specimen table ---\n            cell_table = f['processing']['ophys']['image_segmentation']['cell_specimen_table']\n            valid_roi = cell_table['valid_roi'][()].astype(bool)\n            cell_specimen_ids = cell_table['cell_specimen_id'][()]\n\n            n_valid = valid_roi.sum()\n            if n_valid == 0:\n                print(f\"  WARNING: No valid ROIs in experiment {experiment_id}, skipping\")\n                return None\n\n            # --- Ophys timestamps ---\n            ophys_ts = f['processing']['ophys']['dff']['traces']['timestamps'][()]\n\n            # --- Neural events (filtered by valid_roi) ---\n            events_data = f['processing']['ophys']['event_detection']['data'][()]\n            # events_data shape: (n_timepoints, n_all_cells)\n            events_valid = events_data[:, valid_roi]  # (n_timepoints, n_valid_cells)\n\n            # --- Trials ---\n            trials_grp = f['intervals']['trials']\n            n_trials = len(trials_grp['start_time'][()])\n            trials = {\n                'start_time': trials_grp['start_time'][()],\n                'stop_time': trials_grp['stop_time'][()],\n                'change_time': trials_grp['change_time'][()],\n                'go': trials_grp['go'][()].astype(bool),\n                'catch': trials_grp['catch'][()].astype(bool),\n                'aborted': trials_grp['aborted'][()].astype(bool),\n                'auto_rewarded': trials_grp['auto_rewarded'][()].astype(bool),\n                'hit': trials_grp['hit'][()].astype(bool),\n                'miss': trials_grp['miss'][()].astype(bool),\n                'false_alarm': trials_grp['false_alarm'][()].astype(bool),\n                'correct_reject': trials_grp['correct_reject'][()].astype(bool),\n                'is_change': trials_grp['is_change'][()].astype(bool),\n                'initial_image_name': trials_grp['initial_image_name'][()],\n                'change_image_name': trials_grp['change_image_name'][()],\n            }\n            # Decode bytes\n            if isinstance(trials['initial_image_name'][0], bytes):\n                trials['initial_image_name'] = np.array([x.decode() for x in trials['initial_image_name']])\n                trials['change_image_name'] = np.array([x.decode() for x in trials['change_image_name']])\n\n            # --- Stimulus presentations ---\n            # Find the natural images presentation table\n            stim_key = None\n            for k in f['intervals'].keys():\n                if k != 'trials' and 'spontaneous' not in k.lower() and 'movie' not in k.lower():\n                    stim_key = k\n                    break\n\n            if stim_key is None:\n                print(f\"  WARNING: No stimulus presentations found in {experiment_id}, skipping\")\n                return None\n\n            stim = f['intervals'][stim_key]\n            stim_data = {\n                'start_time': stim['start_time'][()],\n                'stop_time': stim['stop_time'][()],\n                'image_name': stim['image_name'][()],\n                'is_change': stim['is_change'][()].astype(bool),\n                'omitted': stim['omitted'][()],\n            }\n            if isinstance(stim_data['image_name'][0], bytes):\n                stim_data['image_name'] = np.array([x.decode() for x in stim_data['image_name']])\n\n            # Handle omitted field (might be float)\n            if stim_data['omitted'].dtype == float:\n                stim_data['omitted'] = stim_data['omitted'].astype(bool)\n\n            # --- Running speed ---\n            running_speed = f['processing']['running']['speed']['data'][()]\n            running_ts = f['processing']['running']['speed']['timestamps'][()]\n\n            # --- Pupil tracking ---\n            pupil_area = None\n            pupil_ts = None\n            likely_blink = None\n            try:\n                pupil_tracking = f['acquisition']['EyeTracking']['pupil_tracking']\n                pupil_area = pupil_tracking['area'][()]\n                pupil_ts = pupil_tracking['timestamps'][()]\n                likely_blink = f['acquisition']['EyeTracking']['likely_blink']['data'][()].astype(bool)\n            except (KeyError, Exception):\n                print(f\"  WARNING: No pupil tracking data in {experiment_id}\")\n\n            return {\n                'experiment_id': experiment_id,\n                'valid_roi': valid_roi,\n                'cell_specimen_ids': cell_specimen_ids[valid_roi],\n                'n_valid_cells': n_valid,\n                'ophys_ts': ophys_ts,\n                'events': events_valid,\n                'trials': trials,\n                'stim': stim_data,\n                'running_speed': running_speed,\n                'running_ts': running_ts,\n                'pupil_area': pupil_area,\n                'pupil_ts': pupil_ts,\n                'likely_blink': likely_blink,\n            }\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  ERROR loading {experiment_id}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Trial Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_image_at_timepoints(timepoints, stim_data, all_image_names):\n    \"\"\"For each timepoint, determine which image is currently being shown.\n\n    Returns array of image indices (into all_image_names) for each timepoint.\n    During gray screen periods, returns the last shown image.\n    For omitted flashes, continues with the previous image.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_tp = len(timepoints)\n    image_idx = np.zeros(n_tp, dtype=np.int64)\n\n    # Build a sorted list of (start_time, image_name) for non-omitted stimuli\n    non_omitted = ~stim_data['omitted']\n    stim_starts = stim_data['start_time'][non_omitted]\n    stim_names = stim_data['image_name'][non_omitted]\n\n    # Map image names to indices\n    name_to_idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(all_image_names)}\n\n    # For each timepoint, find the most recent stimulus onset\n    # Use searchsorted for efficiency\n    insert_idx = np.searchsorted(stim_starts, timepoints, side='right') - 1\n\n    for i in range(n_tp):\n        if insert_idx[i] >= 0:\n            img_name = stim_names[insert_idx[i]]\n            image_idx[i] = name_to_idx.get(img_name, 0)\n        else:\n            image_idx[i] = 0  # Before first stimulus\n\n    return image_idx\n\n\ndef get_image_change_at_timepoints(timepoints, stim_data):\n    \"\"\"For each timepoint, determine if an image change is occurring.\n\n    Returns binary array: 1 during the first flash after a change, 0 otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_tp = len(timepoints)\n    change_signal = np.zeros(n_tp, dtype=np.int64)\n\n    # Get change stimulus presentations (non-omitted, is_change=True)\n    change_mask = stim_data['is_change'] & ~stim_data['omitted']\n    change_starts = stim_data['start_time'][change_mask]\n    change_stops = stim_data['stop_time'][change_mask]\n\n    # For each change, mark timepoints within the 750ms image presentation interval\n    for cs, ce in zip(change_starts, change_stops):\n        # Mark the full image interval (stimulus + gray) as change\n        # Use 750ms window from change start\n        mask = (timepoints >= cs) & (timepoints < cs + 0.75)\n        change_signal[mask] = 1\n\n    return change_signal\n\n\ndef process_single_experiment(raw_data, exp_meta, target_rate=TARGET_RATE_HZ):\n    \"\"\"Process a single experiment into trial-segmented format.\n\n    Args:\n        raw_data: dict from load_experiment_data\n        exp_meta: dict with 'targeted_structure', 'mouse_id' etc.\n        target_rate: target sampling rate in Hz\n\n    Returns:\n        dict with processed trial data, or None if processing fails.\n    \"\"\"\n    trials = raw_data['trials']\n\n    # Filter trials: keep Go and Catch, exclude Aborted and Auto-rewarded\n    valid_mask = (trials['go'] | trials['catch']) & ~trials['aborted'] & ~trials['auto_rewarded']\n    valid_indices = np.where(valid_mask)[0]\n\n    if len(valid_indices) < 2:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: Only {len(valid_indices)} valid trials in experiment {raw_data['experiment_id']}, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Resample neural events to target rate ---\n    ophys_ts = raw_data['ophys_ts']\n    events = raw_data['events']  # (n_timepoints, n_cells)\n\n    # Create regular timestamp grid at target rate\n    t_start = ophys_ts[0]\n    t_end = ophys_ts[-1]\n    dt = 1.0 / target_rate\n    regular_ts = np.arange(t_start, t_end, dt)\n\n    # Interpolate events to regular grid\n    events_resampled = interpolate_to_regular_grid(ophys_ts, events, regular_ts)\n    # Ensure non-negative (events should be >= 0)\n    events_resampled = np.maximum(events_resampled, 0)\n\n    # --- Resample running speed ---\n    running_resampled = np.interp(regular_ts, raw_data['running_ts'], raw_data['running_speed'])\n\n    # --- Resample pupil area ---\n    pupil_resampled = None\n    if raw_data['pupil_area'] is not None:\n        pupil_area = raw_data['pupil_area'].copy().astype(float)\n        pupil_ts = raw_data['pupil_ts']\n        likely_blink = raw_data['likely_blink']\n\n        # Set blink frames to NaN\n        if likely_blink is not None:\n            pupil_area[likely_blink] = np.nan\n\n        # Interpolate NaNs in pupil area before resampling\n        pupil_area = interpolate_nans(pupil_area)\n\n        # Resample to regular grid\n        pupil_resampled = np.interp(regular_ts, pupil_ts, pupil_area)\n\n    # --- Get all unique image names (excluding 'omitted') ---\n    all_stim_images = sorted(set(\n        raw_data['stim']['image_name'][raw_data['stim']['image_name'] != 'omitted']\n    ))\n\n    # --- Process each valid trial ---\n    neural_trials = []\n    output_trials = []\n    trial_outcomes = []\n\n    for trial_idx in valid_indices:\n        t_trial_start = trials['start_time'][trial_idx]\n        t_trial_stop = trials['stop_time'][trial_idx]\n\n        # Find regular grid indices within this trial\n        trial_mask = (regular_ts >= t_trial_start) & (regular_ts < t_trial_stop)\n        trial_time_indices = np.where(trial_mask)[0]\n\n        if len(trial_time_indices) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        trial_ts = regular_ts[trial_time_indices]\n        n_tp = len(trial_ts)\n\n        # Neural data: (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n        neural_trial = events_resampled[trial_time_indices, :].T.astype(np.float32)\n\n        # Image identity at each timepoint\n        image_idx = get_image_at_timepoints(trial_ts, raw_data['stim'], all_stim_images)\n\n        # Image change signal\n        change_signal = get_image_change_at_timepoints(trial_ts, raw_data['stim'])\n\n        # Running speed (already resampled)\n        running_trial = running_resampled[trial_time_indices]\n\n        # Pupil area (already resampled)\n        if pupil_resampled is not None:\n            pupil_trial = pupil_resampled[trial_time_indices]\n        else:\n            pupil_trial = np.full(n_tp, np.nan)\n\n        # Trial outcome (static)\n        if trials['hit'][trial_idx]:\n            outcome = 0  # hit\n        elif trials['miss'][trial_idx]:\n            outcome = 1  # miss\n        elif trials['false_alarm'][trial_idx]:\n            outcome = 2  # false_alarm\n        elif trials['correct_reject'][trial_idx]:\n            outcome = 3  # correct_reject\n        else:\n            continue  # Unknown outcome, skip\n\n        neural_trials.append(neural_trial)\n        # Store raw values for now; discretization happens after collecting all data\n        output_trials.append({\n            'image_identity': image_idx,\n            'image_change': change_signal,\n            'running_speed': running_trial.astype(np.float32),\n            'pupil_area': pupil_trial.astype(np.float32),\n            'trial_outcome': outcome,\n        })\n\n    if len(neural_trials) < 2:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: Only {len(neural_trials)} processed trials in experiment {raw_data['experiment_id']}, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    return {\n        'experiment_id': raw_data['experiment_id'],\n        'neural_trials': neural_trials,\n        'output_trials': output_trials,\n        'n_cells': raw_data['n_valid_cells'],\n        'all_image_names': all_stim_images,\n        'targeted_structure': exp_meta['targeted_structure'],\n        'mouse_id': str(exp_meta['mouse_id']),\n    }\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Conversion\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_experiment_list(sample=False):\n    \"\"\"Get list of active experiment IDs with metadata.\"\"\"\n    exp_table = pd.read_csv(META_DIR / 'ophys_experiment_table.csv')\n\n    # Get NWB files on disk\n    nwb_files = list(NWB_DIR.glob('*.nwb'))\n    nwb_ids = set()\n    for f in nwb_files:\n        try:\n            eid = int(f.stem.split('_')[-1])\n            nwb_ids.add(eid)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    # Filter to on-disk, active sessions\n    mask = (\n        exp_table['ophys_experiment_id'].isin(nwb_ids) &\n        exp_table['session_type'].isin(ACTIVE_SESSION_TYPES)\n    )\n    active_exps = exp_table[mask].copy()\n\n    if sample:\n        # Pick 2 sessions (preferably from different mice, different equipment)\n        # Pick one single-plane and one multi-plane if possible\n        single = active_exps[active_exps['equipment_name'].str.startswith('CAM')]\n        if len(single) > 0:\n            first = single.iloc[0]\n        else:\n            first = active_exps.iloc[0]\n\n        remaining = active_exps[active_exps['mouse_id'] != first['mouse_id']]\n        if len(remaining) > 0:\n            second = remaining.iloc[0]\n        else:\n            second = active_exps.iloc[1] if len(active_exps) > 1 else first\n\n        active_exps = active_exps[\n            active_exps['ophys_experiment_id'].isin([\n                first['ophys_experiment_id'],\n                second['ophys_experiment_id']\n            ])\n        ]\n\n    print(f\"Selected {len(active_exps)} experiments from {active_exps['mouse_id'].nunique()} mice\")\n    return active_exps\n\n\ndef convert_all(exp_table, show_processing=False):\n    \"\"\"Convert all experiments to the target format.\"\"\"\n\n    all_neural = []\n    all_input = []\n    all_output = []\n    all_subject_idx = []\n    all_brain_region_idx = []\n\n    subjects = sorted(exp_table['mouse_id'].unique().astype(str))\n    subject_to_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    brain_regions = sorted(exp_table['targeted_structure'].unique())\n    region_to_idx = {r: i for i, r in enumerate(brain_regions)}\n\n    # Collect all image names across experiments to build a global mapping\n    all_image_names_set = set()\n\n    # First pass: determine global image names\n    print(\"\\n--- Pass 1: Collecting global image names ---\")\n    t_start_global = time.time()\n\n    # Quick scan of a few files to get image names\n    for _, row in exp_table.iterrows():\n        eid = row['ophys_experiment_id']\n        nwb_path = NWB_DIR / f'behavior_ophys_experiment_{eid}.nwb'\n        try:\n            with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n                for k in f['intervals'].keys():\n                    if k != 'trials' and 'spontaneous' not in k.lower() and 'movie' not in k.lower():\n                        img_names = f['intervals'][k]['image_name'][()]\n                        if isinstance(img_names[0], bytes):\n                            img_names = [x.decode() for x in img_names]\n                        all_image_names_set.update([n for n in img_names if n != 'omitted'])\n                        break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  Error scanning {eid}: {e}\")\n\n    global_image_names = sorted(all_image_names_set)\n    print(f\"  Global image names ({len(global_image_names)}): {global_image_names}\")\n\n    # --- Collect all running speed and pupil values for global percentile computation ---\n    print(\"\\n--- Pass 2: Collecting running speed and pupil data for percentile bins ---\")\n    all_running_values = []\n    all_pupil_values = []\n\n    for idx, (_, row) in enumerate(exp_table.iterrows()):\n        eid = row['ophys_experiment_id']\n        nwb_path = NWB_DIR / f'behavior_ophys_experiment_{eid}.nwb'\n        try:\n            with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n                running = f['processing']['running']['speed']['data'][()]\n                all_running_values.append(running.astype(np.float32))\n\n                try:\n                    pupil_area = f['acquisition']['EyeTracking']['pupil_tracking']['area'][()].astype(float)\n                    blink = f['acquisition']['EyeTracking']['likely_blink']['data'][()].astype(bool)\n                    pupil_area[blink] = np.nan\n                    valid_pupil = pupil_area[~np.isnan(pupil_area)]\n                    if len(valid_pupil) > 0:\n                        all_pupil_values.append(valid_pupil.astype(np.float32))\n                except (KeyError, Exception):\n                    pass\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  Error collecting stats for {eid}: {e}\")\n\n    all_running_cat = np.concatenate(all_running_values) if all_running_values else np.array([0.0])\n    all_pupil_cat = np.concatenate(all_pupil_values) if all_pupil_values else np.array([0.0])\n\n    running_bin_edges = compute_percentile_bins(all_running_cat, n_bins=5)\n    pupil_bin_edges = compute_percentile_bins(all_pupil_cat, n_bins=5)\n\n    print(f\"  Running speed bin edges: {running_bin_edges}\")\n    print(f\"  Pupil area bin edges: {pupil_bin_edges}\")\n    del all_running_values, all_pupil_values, all_running_cat, all_pupil_cat\n\n    # --- Main processing pass ---\n    print(\"\\n--- Pass 3: Processing experiments ---\")\n\n    total_trials = 0\n    total_neurons = 0\n    experiments_processed = 0\n\n    for idx, (_, row) in enumerate(exp_table.iterrows()):\n        eid = row['ophys_experiment_id']\n        nwb_path = NWB_DIR / f'behavior_ophys_experiment_{eid}.nwb'\n\n        t0 = time.time()\n        print(f\"\\n[{idx+1}/{len(exp_table)}] Processing experiment {eid}...\", flush=True)\n\n        # Load raw data\n        raw_data = load_experiment_data(nwb_path, eid)\n        if raw_data is None:\n            continue\n\n        t_load = time.time() - t0\n\n        # Process into trials\n        exp_meta = {\n            'targeted_structure': row['targeted_structure'],\n            'mouse_id': row['mouse_id'],\n        }\n        result = process_single_experiment(raw_data, exp_meta)\n        if result is None:\n            continue\n\n        t_process = time.time() - t0 - t_load\n\n        # --- Build output arrays with global image names and discretization ---\n        session_neural = []\n        session_output = []\n        session_input = []\n\n        # Map local image names to global indices\n        local_to_global = {}\n        for local_idx, name in enumerate(result['all_image_names']):\n            if name in global_image_names:\n                local_to_global[local_idx] = global_image_names.index(name)\n            else:\n                local_to_global[local_idx] = 0\n\n        for trial_data_neural, trial_data_out in zip(result['neural_trials'], result['output_trials']):\n            n_tp = trial_data_neural.shape[1]\n\n            # Neural\n            session_neural.append(trial_data_neural)\n\n            # Input: empty (no inputs for this task)\n            session_input.append(np.zeros((0, n_tp), dtype=np.float32))\n\n            # Output: 5 variables\n            # 1. Image identity (categorical, time-varying)\n            img_id_global = np.array([local_to_global.get(v, 0) for v in trial_data_out['image_identity']], dtype=np.int64)\n\n            # 2. Image change (binary, time-varying)\n            img_change = trial_data_out['image_change'].astype(np.int64)\n\n            # 3. Running speed (discretized, time-varying)\n            running_binned = digitize_to_bins(trial_data_out['running_speed'], running_bin_edges)\n\n            # 4. Pupil diameter (discretized, time-varying)\n            pupil_binned = digitize_to_bins(trial_data_out['pupil_area'], pupil_bin_edges)\n\n            # 5. Trial outcome (static)\n            trial_outcome = np.array([trial_data_out['trial_outcome']], dtype=np.int64)\n\n            # Stack outputs: (n_output, n_timepoints) for time-varying, (n_output,) for static\n            # For mixed time-varying and static, we need to handle carefully\n            # Time-varying outputs: shape (n_output_tv, n_timepoints)\n            # Static outputs: shape (n_output_static,)\n            # Combined: (n_output, n_timepoints) where static is broadcast\n            output_tv = np.stack([img_id_global, img_change, running_binned, pupil_binned], axis=0)  # (4, n_tp)\n            # Append trial outcome as constant across time\n            outcome_broadcast = np.full((1, n_tp), trial_data_out['trial_outcome'], dtype=np.int64)\n            output_combined = np.concatenate([output_tv, outcome_broadcast], axis=0)  # (5, n_tp)\n\n            session_output.append(output_combined)\n\n        all_neural.append(session_neural)\n        all_input.append(session_input)\n        all_output.append(session_output)\n        all_subject_idx.append(subject_to_idx[str(row['mouse_id'])])\n\n        # Brain region index for each neuron\n        region_idx = np.full(result['n_cells'], region_to_idx[row['targeted_structure']], dtype=np.int64)\n        all_brain_region_idx.append(region_idx)\n\n        total_trials += len(session_neural)\n        total_neurons += result['n_cells']\n        experiments_processed += 1\n\n        t_total = time.time() - t0\n        print(f\"  Loaded in {t_load:.1f}s, processed in {t_process:.1f}s, total {t_total:.1f}s\")\n        print(f\"  {result['n_cells']} neurons, {len(session_neural)} trials\")\n\n        # Show processing plots for first 2 sessions\n        if show_processing and experiments_processed <= 2:\n            plot_processing(raw_data, result, session_output, global_image_names,\n                          running_bin_edges, pupil_bin_edges, eid)\n\n    print(f\"\\n=== Conversion Summary ===\")\n    print(f\"Experiments processed: {experiments_processed}\")\n    print(f\"Total trials: {total_trials}\")\n    print(f\"Total neurons: {total_neurons}\")\n    print(f\"Subjects: {len(subjects)}\")\n    print(f\"Brain regions: {brain_regions}\")\n\n    # Build output names and values\n    output_names = ['image_identity', 'image_change', 'running_speed', 'pupil_diameter', 'trial_outcome']\n\n    output_values = [\n        global_image_names,  # image identity values\n        ['no_change', 'change'],  # image change values\n        [f'bin_{i}' for i in range(5)],  # running speed bins\n        [f'bin_{i}' for i in range(5)],  # pupil diameter bins\n        ['hit', 'miss', 'false_alarm', 'correct_reject'],  # trial outcomes\n    ]\n\n    data = {\n        'neural': all_neural,\n        'input': all_input,\n        'output': all_output,\n        'subjects': [str(s) for s in subjects],\n        'subject_idx': np.array(all_subject_idx, dtype=np.int64),\n        'brain_regions': brain_regions,\n        'brain_region_idx': all_brain_region_idx,\n        'input_names': [],\n        'output_names': output_names,\n        'output_values': output_values,\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description': 'Visual change detection task: decode image identity, image change, running speed, pupil diameter, and trial outcome from neural activity',\n            'time_bin_size': TIME_BIN_MS,\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'Trial start time (first stimulus onset of trial)',\n            'off_start': 0.0,\n            'off_end': None,\n            'target_rate_hz': TARGET_RATE_HZ,\n            'neural_signal': 'calcium events (FastLZeroSpikeInference)',\n            'running_speed_bin_edges': running_bin_edges.tolist(),\n            'pupil_area_bin_edges': pupil_bin_edges.tolist(),\n            'active_session_types': ACTIVE_SESSION_TYPES,\n            'trial_filter': 'Go and Catch trials only (excluded Aborted and Auto-rewarded)',\n            'valid_roi_filter': True,\n        }\n    }\n\n    return data\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Visualization\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef plot_processing(raw_data, result, session_output, global_image_names,\n                    running_bin_edges, pupil_bin_edges, eid):\n    \"\"\"Plot processing steps for a single experiment.\"\"\"\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(6, 2, figsize=(24, 20))\n    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: Experiment {eid}', fontsize=14)\n\n    ophys_ts = raw_data['ophys_ts']\n    events = raw_data['events']\n\n    # Plot 1: Raw events (first 5 neurons, first 60s)\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    t_mask = ophys_ts < ophys_ts[0] + 60\n    n_show = min(5, events.shape[1])\n    for i in range(n_show):\n        ax.plot(ophys_ts[t_mask], events[t_mask, i] + i*0.5, alpha=0.7, linewidth=0.5)\n    ax.set_title('Raw events (first 60s, first 5 neurons)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time (s)')\n\n    # Plot 2: Trial structure\n    ax = axes[0, 1]\n    trials = raw_data['trials']\n    valid = (trials['go'] | trials['catch']) & ~trials['aborted'] & ~trials['auto_rewarded']\n    go_valid = trials['go'] & valid\n    catch_valid = trials['catch'] & valid\n\n    for i in np.where(go_valid)[0][:50]:\n        ax.axvspan(trials['start_time'][i], trials['stop_time'][i], alpha=0.3, color='blue')\n    for i in np.where(catch_valid)[0][:50]:\n        ax.axvspan(trials['start_time'][i], trials['stop_time'][i], alpha=0.3, color='red')\n    ax.set_title('Trial structure (blue=Go, red=Catch)')\n    ax.set_xlim(trials['start_time'][valid][0], trials['start_time'][valid][min(49, valid.sum()-1)])\n\n    # What processing is involved in computing `output` *Image identity*?\n\ni. For each timepoint, the most recent non-omitted stimulus onset is found via `np.searchsorted`. The image name from that stimulus is mapped to a global index. Global image names are collected across all experiments in a first pass and sorted alphabetically. During gray screen periods, the identity of the last shown image persists. For omitted flashes, the previous image continues.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef get_image_at_timepoints(timepoints, stim_data, all_image_names):\n    non_omitted = ~stim_data['omitted']\n    stim_starts = stim_data['start_time'][non_omitted]\n    stim_names = stim_data['image_name'][non_omitted]\n    name_to_idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(all_image_names)}\n    insert_idx = np.searchsorted(stim_starts, timepoints, side='right') - 1\n    for i in range(n_tp):\n        if insert_idx[i] >= 0:\n            img_name = stim_names[insert_idx[i]]\n            image_idx[i] = name_to_idx.get(img_name, 0)\n        else:\n            image_idx[i] = 0\n    return image_idx\n```\n\niii. The searchsorted approach efficiently finds the most recent stimulus for each timepoint. The global image name mapping ensures consistent indices across sessions.\n\n## 3-c. How is `output` *Image identity* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Image identity is computed at the same 30 Hz regular grid timepoints used for the neural data. Image change is derived from the stimulus presentations table, using `is_change` (boolean indicating actual image changes) and `omitted` flags, combined with `start_time` for temporal localization.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef get_image_change_at_timepoints(timepoints, stim_data):\n    change_mask = stim_data['is_change'] & ~stim_data['omitted']\n    change_starts = stim_data['start_time'][change_mask]\n    change_stops = stim_data['stop_time'][change_mask]\n```\n\niii. `is_change` from stimulus presentations marks actual image changes (equivalent to Go trials).\n\n## 4-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Image change*?\n\ni. A binary signal is constructed: for each stimulus presentation where `is_change=True` and `omitted=False`, a 750ms window starting at the stimulus onset time is marked as 1. All other timepoints are 0.\n\nii.\n```python\nfor cs, ce in zip(change_starts, change_stops):\n    mask = (timepoints >= cs) & (timepoints < cs + 0.75)\n    change_signal[mask] = 1\nreturn change_signal\n```\n\niii. The 750ms window corresponds to one image flash (250ms) plus the following gray inter-stimulus interval (500ms).\n\n## 4-c. How is `output` *Image change* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Image change is inherently binary (0 = no change, 1 = change). No thresholding is needed -- the signal is directly constructed as a binary indicator.\n\nii. See 4-b code snippet. The values are 0 or 1 by construction.\n\niii. N/A -- binary variable by definition.\n\n## 4-d. How is `output` *Image change* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Running speed is linearly interpolated from its native timestamps (~60 Hz) to the 30 Hz regular grid. Global percentile-based bin edges are computed from ALL running speed data across all experiments (full session data, not restricted to trial periods). Running speed is then discretized into 5 bins using these global edges.\n\nii.\n```python\n# Resampling\nrunning_resampled = np.interp(regular_ts, raw_data['running_ts'], raw_data['running_speed'])\n\n# Percentile bin computation (Pass 2 -- full session data)\nrunning = f['processing']['running']['speed']['data'][()]\nall_running_values.append(running.astype(np.float32))\n...\nrunning_bin_edges = compute_percentile_bins(all_running_cat, n_bins=5)\n\n# Discretization\nrunning_binned = digitize_to_bins(trial_data_out['running_speed'], running_bin_edges)\n```\n\niii. Linear interpolation preserves the signal while resampling to the target rate. Percentile-based bins ensure roughly equal class counts.\n\n## 5-c. How is `output` *Running speed* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Running speed is discretized into 5 bins using percentile-based edges computed from all running speed data across all sessions. `np.digitize` maps continuous values to bin indices 0-4. NaN values are mapped to bin 0.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_percentile_bins(values, n_bins=5):\n    valid = values[~np.isnan(values)]\n    percentiles = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)\n    edges = np.percentile(valid, percentiles)\n    for i in range(1, len(edges)):\n        if edges[i] <= edges[i-1]:\n            edges[i] = edges[i-1] + 1e-10\n    return edges\n\ndef digitize_to_bins(values, bin_edges):\n    n_bins = len(bin_edges) - 1\n    binned = np.digitize(values, bin_edges[1:-1])\n    binned = np.clip(binned, 0, n_bins - 1)\n    binned[np.isnan(values)] = 0\n    return binned.astype(np.int64)\n```\n\niii. Percentile binning ensures roughly equal occupancy per bin, which benefits decoder training. The epsilon adjustment handles edge cases where percentile values are identical.\n\n## 5-d. How is `output` *Running speed* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Running speed is interpolated to the same 30 Hz regular grid as neural data before trial segmentation. The same `trial_time_indices` are used to extract both.\n\nii.\n```python\nrunning_resampled = np.interp(regular_ts, raw_data['running_ts'], raw_data['running_speed'])\n...\nrunning_trial = running_resampled[trial_time_indices]\n```\n\niii. Interpolation to the same timebase ensures alignment.\n\n## 6-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Pupil diameter* derived from?\n\ni. The AI used pupil area as a proxy for pupil diameter. The CONVERSION_NOTES state: \"Use pupil area as proxy for diameter.\"\n\n## 6-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Pupil diameter*?\n\ni. Blink frames (where `likely_blink=True`) are set to NaN. Then NaN values are linearly interpolated within the timeseries (filling gaps from blinks). The clean signal is then interpolated to the 30 Hz regular grid. Global percentile bins are computed from non-blink pupil area data across all experiments (full session, not trial-restricted). The signal is then discretized into 5 bins.\n\nii.\n```python\npupil_area[likely_blink] = np.nan\npupil_area = interpolate_nans(pupil_area)  # Fill NaN gaps by interpolation\npupil_resampled = np.interp(regular_ts, pupil_ts, pupil_area)\n\n# Percentile computation (Pass 2)\npupil_area = f['acquisition']['EyeTracking']['pupil_tracking']['area'][()].astype(float)\nblink = f['acquisition']['EyeTracking']['likely_blink']['data'][()].astype(bool)\npupil_area[blink] = np.nan\nvalid_pupil = pupil_area[~np.isnan(pupil_area)]\n...\npupil_bin_edges = compute_percentile_bins(all_pupil_cat, n_bins=5)\n```\n\niii. Blink removal prevents artifacts. NaN interpolation fills blink gaps before resampling. Global percentile bins ensure consistent categories.\n\n## 6-c. How is `output` *Pupil diameter* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Linear interpolation preserves the signal while resampling to the target rate. Percentile-based bins ensure roughly equal class counts.\n\n## 5-c. How is `output` *Running speed* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Running speed is discretized into 5 bins using percentile-based edges computed from all running speed data across all sessions. `np.digitize` maps continuous values to bin indices 0-4. NaN values are mapped to bin 0.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_percentile_bins(values, n_bins=5):\n    valid = values[~np.isnan(values)]\n    percentiles = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)\n    edges = np.percentile(valid, percentiles)\n    for i in range(1, len(edges)):\n        if edges[i] <= edges[i-1]:\n            edges[i] = edges[i-1] + 1e-10\n    return edges\n\ndef digitize_to_bins(values, bin_edges):\n    n_bins = len(bin_edges) - 1\n    binned = np.digitize(values, bin_edges[1:-1])\n    binned = np.clip(binned, 0, n_bins - 1)\n    binned[np.isnan(values)] = 0\n    return binned.astype(np.int64)\n```\n\niii. Percentile binning ensures roughly equal occupancy per bin, which benefits decoder training. The epsilon adjustment handles edge cases where percentile values are identical.\n\n## 5-d. How is `output` *Running speed* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Running speed is interpolated to the same 30 Hz regular grid as neural data before trial segmentation. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Pupil diameter*?\n\ni. Blink frames (where `likely_blink=True`) are set to NaN. Several cases are handled:\n- **Failed experiment loading**: If `load_experiment_data` returns None (e.g., no valid ROIs, missing stimulus data), the experiment is skipped.\n- **Missing pupil data**: If eye tracking is not available, pupil values are filled with NaN (then mapped to bin 0 during discretization).\n- **Blink frames**: Set to NaN and linearly interpolated.\n- **Short trials**: Trials with fewer than 3 timepoints after resampling are skipped.\n- **Unknown trial outcomes**: Trials where none of the four outcome flags is True are skipped.\n- **Few valid trials**: Experiments with fewer than 2 valid processed trials are skipped.\n- **Non-negative events**: Events are clipped to >= 0 after interpolation.\n\nii.\n```python\nif raw_data is None:\n    continue\n...\nif len(trial_time_indices) < 3:\n    continue\n...\nelse:\n    continue  # Unknown outcome, skip\n...\nif len(neural_trials) < 2:\n    return None\n...\nevents_resampled = np.maximum(events_resampled, 0)\n...\nif raw_data['pupil_area'] is not None:\n    ...\nelse:\n    pupil_trial = np.full(n_tp, np.nan)\n```\n\niii.
